## Phase P4: Contract layer (docs/21 §3.3 P4, §5.5)

**Entry**: WP-30 backend audit accepted at G-AUDIT (WP-39R GO); WP-13 real-service stack running; WP-05 evidence recorder available.
**Exit**: contract inventory CT-1..CT-22 (docs/01 §14) each with a test on both sides or a recorded n/a; every client drift mismatch registered as a finding; HC-4 held for compatibility-window changes.

**Phase-group conventions (P4-P5)**:

- **Paths**: `$SPEC` = `$FEAT` = `specs/001-full-project-audit-remediation`; `$EV` = `$SPEC/evidence`; `$AUD` = `$SPEC/audit`. Every `[REVIEW]` verdict is written to `$EV/reviews/<WP>[-<item>][-r<n>].json` (one folder for the whole task list; `-r<n>` is a later review iteration, review verdict row of the abbreviation table), and the merge-review file of a merge commit that CPA itself makes is `$EV/reviews/CPA-merge-<run_id>.json`, its name written by CPA into that commit's `Awaits-Review:` line (T040, T042; Diverged repository bullet); human-checkpoint records go to `$EV/hc/HC-<n>[-<USn>].json`. A path marked `(new)` does not exist yet and is a deliverable of the task that names it (or of the task named in the mark); generated evidence files under `$EV/` and `$AUD/` are all new and are not marked one by one, and paths created by P0-P3 tasks (for example `scripts/commit-push-all.sh`, `tools/evidence/*`, `scripts/build/verify_artifact.sh`, `scripts/register/gate.sh`, `scripts/qa/run_profile.sh`, `$SPEC/matrix/applicability.yaml`, `docs/DOC_SCOPE.yaml`) are not marked here. A new path under a directory that `.gitignore` ignores today (`tools/`, `build/`, `coverage/`) depends on the WP-09 `.gitignore` negation task of P0; paths that no negation covers (`reports/`, `qa-results/`, any `coverage/` directory below the root) are not used here. Top-level `$EV` entries first written in P4-P7 (all listed in the docs/06 §11 layout), each written only by the tasks named: `exceptions/` (T440; T582 only reads it), `flake_ledger.jsonl` (T563), `p5-exit.json` (T457), `p6-exit.json` (T557), `pack/` (T588, T589), `qa/` (T462, T463, T474; also P2 WP-24), `register/` (T576; also P2 WP-20), `release_digests/` (T448), `reproducibility/` (T443), `retest/` (T567, T570), `sbom/` (T445), `verify/` (T581, T582), `wp73/` (T580b, the evidence of the final constitution update). No `$EV` or `$AUD` path is written by the commit-push script (next bullets).
- **Identifiers**: `CT-n` is contract n of the docs/01 §14 inventory (written `Cn` there); `BC-n` is backend candidate n of the docs/07 candidate table (also written `Cn` there). The doc12 conversion classes C1..C6 keep their doc12 names.
- **Containerized runner (TIC)**: `scripts/test-in-container.sh <app> <lane> -- <cmd>` (created in WP-12 (T121), a dispatcher over the T120 wrappers `scripts/containers/run_go.sh`, `run_node.sh`, `run_docs.sh`, `run_scan.sh`, `run_playwright.sh`, plus `scripts/containers/run_qa.sh` for the rows of the `qa` app, which run in IMG-QA, the WP-24 QA image of T211 (helixqa, Tesseract, ffmpeg, Playwright browsers); `run_qa.sh` and the `qa` rows of `scripts/containers/lanes.tsv` are delivered by T212, on which every `TIC qa` call depends, named in each such task together with T211; lanes `unit`, `contract`, `integration`, `e2e`, `api`, `docs`, `tooling`; digest-pinned images from `build/containers/images.lock.yaml`). The rows of the `tooling` app (`TIC tooling <lane>`, called by T309 to T312, T319, T324, T420, T435a, T453, T510, T514, T553, T554, T570 and T584) run in IMG-TESTUTIL through `run_testutil.sh`, the wrapper the WP-12 lane table names for that app key (abbreviation table); that image must carry python3, pytest, PyYAML, jsonschema, bash, git and jq, proven by a smoke run of `python3 -c 'import pytest, yaml, jsonschema'` and `jq --version` inside it, and a `TIC tooling` RED counts only after that smoke has passed in the same image (a RED caused by a missing interpreter or module is a RED for the wrong reason, §11.4.224(A)); the WP-60 converter tests use `TIC qa tooling` in IMG-QA instead (T460). The rows of the `docs` app (`TIC docs docs`, T520, T542, T545, T546 and T547; the Python validator of T533 runs in the same image) run in IMG-DOCS through `run_docs.sh`, which therefore needs python3 and pytest besides its render tools (docs/16 §6.1 lists them in the IMG-DOCS row), under the same smoke-before-RED rule. Every build of a deliverable artifact (Go binary, web image, Rust bundle, Android APK) runs in its build container on the remote build host through the WP-14 mechanism (`build/hosts.env`, `build/components.json`, `cmd/distributed-build`) and returns the artifact with a sha256 manifest checked by `scripts/build/verify_artifact.sh`; when the host is unavailable the build is BLOCKED-ON ODG-07 (input, cannot be defaulted), never moved to the bare host. No build or test runs on the bare host (§11.4.173, FR-021).
- **Evidence**: every run goes through `tools/evidence/evrec` into `$EV/ledger.jsonl` as an `ev/1` record (`$SPEC/contracts/evidence-record.schema.json`); item verdicts are derived by `tools/evidence/verdict` into `$EV/verdicts/<ATM-id>.json`, never written by hand (review verdicts never go there).
- **Commit-push outputs and clean-tree checks** (abbreviation table: `$CPA_RUN`, CPA run record, clean tracked tree): the commit-push script (CPA, WP-04, T042) writes nothing into the tracked tree and commits none of its own outputs: its `report.json`, `summary.txt`, S7 verifier JSON `verify.json`, stage transcripts, deferral rows, the §9.2 backup of a merge integration under `backup/` (a `git bundle` of the local range checked by `git bundle verify`, plus a copy of every uncommitted file under `backup/worktree/` with its sha256) and the conflicting files of a refused merge under `conflicts/` stay in its ignored run directory `$CPA_RUN` = `.audit/commit-push/<run_id>/` (never tmpfs; `<run_id>` = UTC time, process id and a random suffix); besides that directory a run that holds the lock writes only two other ignored places, its lock and long-op registry records under `.audit/longops/` (T089) and the pending-pin rows of `.audit/pending_pins.tsv` (T042a), and a run that does not get the lock writes only its own run directory and touches nothing else. Git itself is the tracked record of a push: every commit CPA makes carries the trailer `CPA-Run: <run_id>` and, in its message body, its deferral flags (`Deferred-Gates:` with `SKIP_LONG`, `SWEEP_ABSENT` or `LOCAL_ONLY`), on a held commit `Awaits-Review: <verdict path>`, and `Foreign-Commit: <sha>` lines naming the commits without a `CPA-Run` trailer that S1 brought in from a live remote tip (only commits reachable from a live remote tip at S1 are ever named; a trailer-less local commit that no remote holds, such as a plain `git commit` outside CPA, is refused with 20 and never named, T042). A task that needs a run's report or verifier result as evidence cites the CPA run record: after the run it captures `$CPA_RUN/report.json` (and `verify.json` where needed) with `tools/evidence/evrec`, an `ev/1` entry whose blob `$EV/blobs/<sha256>` is the file byte for byte, and that entry and blob are ordinary members of the task's next declared change set. Shared stores (`$EV/ledger.jsonl`, `$EV/blobs/`) belong to the change set of whichever task wrote to them; a CPA run refuses only untracked or modified files outside its declared change set (T042). Every clean-tree or `dirty` condition of P4-P7 (T429, T442, T579a, T582, T595b) is measured on the clean tracked tree of the abbreviation table: `scripts/repo/verify_repos.sh --no-remote` reports `summary.dirty` equal to `summary.dirty_excepted`, each repository judged by `git status --porcelain --ignore-submodules=all` (untracked files counted, ignored paths such as `.audit/` never), excepted rows coming only from the reviewed `scripts/repo/exceptions.tsv` (T032; the docling `dirty` row, permanently modified CRLF test data, is such a row), pin drift reported beside it and matched against `.audit/pending_pins.tsv`; no test or gate of P4-P7 asserts a raw `git status --porcelain` on the main repository, which also prints a dirty or moved submodule; and the measurement cites, by its report sha256, the CPA run record of the run that made the measured HEAD (its run id read from the `CPA-Run:` trailer of that commit), or records `report_sha256: null` with a reason when HEAD is not a CPA commit or that report is absent on this host (clean tracked tree row of the abbreviation table).
- **Generated tracked files**: the container-outputs rule of the P0-P1 conventions applies (a containerized step writes only to `/out`, RUNP `--out <dir>`, default the ignored `.audit/out/<op_id>/`; no container writes `/src` except `scripts/register/locked.sh` through `RUNP --rw docs`, T064). In P4-P7 every tracked file a containerized step generates or edits (a route baseline or dump, a drift or contract report, a converted bank file, a regenerated bank-id floor, a wave assignment, a dependency report, a schema dump, an index, a diagram render, an export twin, a badge or a generated reference; for example T312, T314, T319, T453, T454, T462, T463 to T472, T523, T534 to T537, T541, T542 and T545) is written under `/out` at its repository-relative path, and a host step of the same task, after the container has exited, copies each such file to that path and records the `/out` source and the tracked destination with their sha256, which must be equal, in the task's `ev/1` entry; a test that asserts a generated file asserts it under `/out` (or under the output variable that points there). The copy is a file operation, not a build or a test, so it does not breach the no-bare-host rule.
- **Commit windows (parallel streams)**: the P0-P1 commit-window rule applies (§11.4.84): the working tree is shared by every stream and CPA refuses an uncommitted file its declared change set does not name, so at a window every stream stops writing, releases its long-op registrations and hands the conductor (ST-GOV) its uncommitted paths, each with the review verdict it still waits for, and the conductor runs one CPA run over their union as `--paths-from` (T042); a stream never declares another stream's files to get past a refusal, and a `--repo <path>` run, whose S0 sweep is scoped to `<path>` (T042a), is not stopped by another stream's uncommitted files elsewhere. A file that open tasks of two streams edit is held by one stream at a time, because a window commits a path on one verdict only, so one stream's change to it would otherwise ride on the other stream's review: in P5 this is `catalog-api/main.go`, edited at least by the WP-50 change sets of T339, T341, T343, T350 and T353 (ST-SEC) and by T368, T376 and, where its envelope change reaches that file, T375 of WP-51 (ST-API); the conductor grants the file to one of the two streams and records the holder in the `ev/1` entry of the window run, and the other stream starts its edit only after the holder's change to the file is committed and released by its review's GO, or undone by the NO-GO procedure (Review before push bullet).
- **Fix protocol (FP)** for every [TDD] fix task in P5 (docs/06 §4, §11.4.115(F)): (1) root-cause note on the register item (`docs/workable_items.db`, FR-008, §11.4.102); (2) RED `ev/1` entry on the pre-fix artifact fingerprint, observed before the fix is written; (3) the fix; (4) GREEN on a different artifact fingerprint, three identical GREEN iterations; (5) reviewer-authored mutation recorded as a caught `MUTATION` entry (§11.4.194(6)(d)); (6) clean-target verification of the built artifact (FR-021, §11.4.200); (7) commit through `scripts/commit-push-all.sh` (CPA) on `main` only, no force-push (FR-024, §11.4.113). Work committed inside a submodule repository (T430, T431, T433, T499, T501, T572 where the strengthened test lives in a submodule, and the engine gitlink commit that T580b makes inside the constitution repository) uses the per-repository mode `CPA --repo <path>` of WP-04 (T042a): it commits in, and pushes from, that repository only, to that repository's remotes as enumerated at run time, and leaves every ancestor repository's HEAD and gitlink unchanged; in main mode a declared held path inside a submodule is refused (20, `hold_in_submodule`, T042), so unreviewed submodule work never rides on a main-repository change set; a gitlink moves only through a pointer commit in the parent that has passed its G-PIN review (docs/11 §6.4 step 7: T441 before the T442 layer commits of WP-55, T580a before the final pointer moves of WP-73 T581 and the constitution update T580b), never as a side effect of a module commit, and a pointer commit never publishes a pin that no remote of that submodule holds (the submodule is pushed first, docs/16 §11.1 R4). Each `--repo` commit records its pending pin move (repository path, new gitlink sha, run id) in the ignored file `.audit/pending_pins.tsv`, and the task that ran that commit run captures its CPA run record with `evrec` (its `report.json` lists the commits made with their run id), so that WP-73 can tell this feature's upstream commits from another actor's from tracked records (WP-73 Inputs); a fast-forward that moves a submodule HEAD without a commit (the WP-55 layer moves of T436 to T439, the late catch-up of T579a and the constitution fast-forward and engine checkouts of T580b) is made together with its row decision in one hold of the CPA lock by the reviewed helper `scripts/repo/record_pending_pin.sh --ff <path> <sha>` (T435a), which writes or replaces the row when the move left HEAD different from the parent's gitlink and removes it when HEAD equals the gitlink, and a rollback of that move is made with its row decision in one lock hold by the helper's `--rollback <path> <old>` mode, which restores a row for the HEAD the rollback returned to while that HEAD still differs from the parent's gitlink (an earlier `--repo` commit of T430, T431, T499 or T572) and removes the row when HEAD equals the gitlink again (the `--after-rollback` decision); a nested third-party checkout that a constitution update moves is brought to its new gitlink in one lock hold by the helper's `--sync-third-party` mode, a nested gitlink that the update adds is initialised in one lock hold by its `--init` mode, and the checkout that a nested gitlink deleted by the update leaves behind is moved, never deleted, to the ignored `.audit/wp73/removed/<path>/` with its sha256 list by its `--retire` mode, none of the three writing a row (T580b (3b′)); a helper call refused because another op holds the lock moves nothing and changes no row, and is re-run, the same command, once the lock is free (each refusal captured in the task's evidence), never replaced by a move made by hand; in P4-P7 that helper is the only writer of rows for moves that are not commits (the integrate-only `--repo` run of T042a is not used for them); a main-repository CPA run reports a pin drift that exactly matches a pending row as pending, not failing, while any other drift still exits 15; the pointer commit of the G-PIN layer (T442, T581, and `CPA --repo <parent>` for a nested parent such as the constitution in T580b) consumes, that is removes, the rows of the pins it moves (T042a); and the S0 sweep of a `--repo` run is scoped to `<path>` (T042a). The same pending rows are what the standalone verifier runs of WP-55 accept as pending (T429, T435, T442).
- **blocked-unavailable**: a test needing an absent real service, credential or device records `verdict: blocked` with `blocked_reason` and `counts_as: not_passing` (FR-025, docs/05 §10); it is never a pass or a skip.
- **Producer is not verifier**: every [REVIEW] task is a pure review owned by ST-REV on Opus at xhigh (§11.4.209, §11.4.240), iterates to zero blocking findings (§11.4.134), writes its verdict to `$EV/reviews/`, and never produces the reviewed work; fixes found by a review go back to the producing stream. Every verdict file has the `review-verdict/1` format of the review verdict row of the abbreviation table, with every field that `$FEAT/contracts/review-verdict.schema.json` requires: `schema` (`review-verdict/1`), `verdict` (`GO` or `NO-GO`), `blocking_findings` (the count of blocking findings, 0 for a GO), `covers_runs` (one entry per commit the review covers: its `CPA-Run` id, which is the key a hold is released on, with its repository path and commit sha, read from the commit trailers; an empty list when no commit awaits the verdict), `model` and `effort` (the model and effort that ran the review); `gate`, `author` and `reviewer` are optional.
- **Review before push**: a change made before the `[REVIEW]` GO that covers it is committed as a held commit: its paths go into the declared change set with that review's verdict file in the second column of the `--paths-from` list (or the whole run is held with `--awaits-review <verdict file>`), so the commit carries `Deferred-Gates: LOCAL_ONLY` and `Awaits-Review: <verdict file>` beside its `CPA-Run` trailer (WP-04 T039, T041, T042; `--repo <path>` runs hold the same way, T042a; in main mode a held path inside a submodule is refused with 20, so submodule work is held through `--repo <path>` and reaches its parent only through a G-PIN pointer move). The verdict path of an `Awaits-Review:` line is always relative to the main-repository root and is always read from the main repository's committed HEAD (`git -C <main root> show HEAD:<path>`), whichever repository holds the commit: a held commit inside a submodule is released by its verdict committed in the main repository, never by a file in the submodule's own tree. S6 of every later CPA run, main-repository and `--repo` alike, pushes a held commit only when that verdict file is committed in the main repository's HEAD with `verdict: GO` and its `covers_runs` lists the held commit's `CPA-Run` id; until then it pushes nothing past the held commit and the run exits 14 with reason `review_pending` naming each held commit and its verdict. So no stream's run publishes another stream's unreviewed commit on the shared `main`, and no task pushes unreviewed work to any remote (§11.4.142, §11.4.125); the owed push is made by the first CPA run after that GO is committed in the main repository (a `--repo` run for a submodule's held commits), or by a later one, and is not a dependency edge of the producing task. A hold on a verdict file that already holds GO in the main repository's HEAD is refused (20, `verdict_already_go`, T042): work made after a GO waits on the verdict file of a new review iteration (`<WP>[-<item>]-r<n>.json`), so an earlier GO never covers later work. A held commit whose review ends in a NO-GO that abandons the change is undone by the NO-GO procedure of the P0-P1 commit-window rule, which governs this bullet: at the next window the producing stream runs `git revert --no-commit <held commits>`, `git revert --quit` and `git restore --staged -- <reverted paths>` (CPA has no revert operation, and a plain `git revert` would make a trailer-less local commit that S1 refuses with 20, `unrecorded_local_commit`), and the conductor commits the reverted paths through CPA, declared and held on the same verdict file (14), never a reset, rebase or force-push (§11.4.113); S6 still pushes nothing past the held commit below it; ST-REV then writes GO into that verdict file, its `covers_runs` naming the held commits and the revert commit and stating that the net change pushed is none, which releases both, so the abandoned change is published only together with its reversal (a GO that names the held commits without the revert is refused at S5 before it is committed, 20, `verdict_covers_incomplete`, T039, T042). Review verdict files and human-checkpoint records are gate records, committed and pushed by ordinary runs. In P5 the commit of FP step 7 is pushed only after the review task of its work package returns GO for that change (T359, T360, T378, T379, T395, T398, T411, T428, T432, T434a, T435b, T441, T451, T456); in P4 the G-CONTRACT (T322) and WP-41 (T336) reviews gate the pushes.
- **Diverged repository** (plan owner's rule (Y); WP-04 T040, T042, T042a): when a remote of a repository moves while that repository holds local commits that no remote has (a held commit awaiting its GO, or a released one not yet pushed), the repository is not left diverged: the next CPA run over it (main-repository or `--repo <path>`), under the CPA lock and after its §9.2 backup under `$CPA_RUN/backup/`, integrates the newest live remote tip itself through `scripts/repo/integrate_merge.sh` (T040) with `git merge --no-ff <live remote tip>`, never a rebase, reset or force-push (§11.4.113); the merge commit carries `CPA-Run:` and one `Foreign-Commit: <sha>` line per trailer-less remote commit it brings in, and in a submodule replaces that repository's pending pin row (T042a). Whenever it merges into a held range (a commit below it carries an `Awaits-Review:` line and is not yet on every remote, whether or not its verdict already holds GO) or resolves conflicts (a merge with a conflict is refused with 12, `merge_conflict`, nothing moved and the conflicting files copied under `$CPA_RUN/conflicts/`; it is made only by a later `--resolve-merge <dir>` run whose resolution, written under `.audit/merge-resolution/<run_id>/` of the refused run (any other directory gives 20, `resolution_dir_invalid`), was made on Opus at xhigh, §11.4.211, T040, T042), it is held on its own merge-review file `$EV/reviews/CPA-merge-<run_id>.json`, which CPA itself writes into its `Awaits-Review:` line (T040, T042), never on the verdict file the held range awaits and never on an `-r<n>` iteration of it. S6 pushes nothing below an unreleased CPA merge commit (T041), and the range is pushed only once both kinds of verdict are GO: each held commit's own verdict, and the merge-review file, which the reviewer of the range the merge went into writes after reviewing the merge commit with its incoming range (only the remote's commits brought in, every held change kept), its `covers_runs` naming the merge run: T432 for the module change sets of T430 and T431 (pushed by T433), T500 for those of T499 (T501), the covering T571 re-draw for the T572 commits in a submodule, T580a for every repository other than the main repository that WP-73 pushes (the constitution included: T580b, T581 (1) and (2)), and the standing merge review T308a for every merge in the main repository (the commit windows of P4 to P7, the G-PIN pointer runs of T442, the WP-73 root runs of T581 and the closing push T595a). That GO is committed in the main repository's HEAD before the run that pushes is re-run: for a merge inside a submodule by a main-repository CPA run made first, for a main-repository merge by an earlier window or by the re-run's own declared change set (T039). A merge made by hand carries no trailer and no remote holds it, so it stays refused with 20 (`unrecorded_local_commit`, T040), a merge left in progress (`MERGE_HEAD` present) is refused at S0 with 20 (`merge_in_progress`; one that a killed CPA run left is attributed through its merge record `merge.json` and named with its remediation, T040, T042, T090), and no P4-P7 task merges outside CPA. Every GO of this task list, a merge-review file's included, lists in `covers_runs` every held commit that awaits its file, or the run that commits it is refused with 20 (`verdict_covers_incomplete`, T042). The held module commits of T430, T431, T499 and T572 and the constitution commit of T580b integrate this way when their upstream moves while they wait (T433, T501, T572, T580b, T581); inside WP-73 each repository gets at most one such merge-and-review round, and a further move of its remote is an ODG-41 row (WP-73 Inputs), never a further round. A foreign push that changes a path which the declared change set of a window also changes stops that run with 12 (`ff_blocked_by_local_changes`, nothing moved); the window is then re-run in the commit-before-integrate mode of T040 and T042: under the CPA lock and after its §9.2 bundle backup, CPA first commits the declared change set as ordinary CPA commits (held where declared) and then runs the merge path, so the overlap becomes a `merge_conflict` that a `--resolve-merge` run settles and the merge is reviewed like any other (in the main repository by the standing merge review T308a). The hash-chained `$EV/ledger.jsonl` and the binary `docs/workable_items.db` each have one writer (`tools/evidence/evrec`; ST-REG through `scripts/register/locked.sh` and the T066 procedure), and a conflict in either is never merged as text: the remote side is kept and the local entries or rows are recorded again on top of it by that writer, a `ledger#<seq>` reference to a re-recorded entry being updated to its new `seq` (T040, T042); the remediation of every code-12 reason is the one `docs/scripts/commit-push-all.md` gives (T045).
- **Register writes**: every mutating `$WI` or `sqlite3` command on `docs/workable_items.db` runs through `scripts/register/locked.sh -- <cmd>` (T064), which holds `flock docs/.register.lock` around exactly one command (single writer, doc04 §12.2; a bare mutating call is a review finding), in IMG-TESTUTIL (the image T008 maps to `sqlite3`); read-only queries use the `immutable=1` URI (T071) under the read precondition of the P0-P1 conventions: a read is either one `locked.sh` call that runs `PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)` and then the query, or a reader that refuses (20) while `docs/workable_items.db-wal` is non-empty, because an `immutable=1` reader does not see rows still in the WAL (docs/04 §12.2; 2 rows where a `mode=ro` reader saw 3); the T066 dump-and-commit procedure ends every committed write with that checkpoint, and a read-only step of a clean-tree or release seam (the release-seam checks of T569 and T582) is always a refusing reader, never a checkpointing one, because a checkpoint of uncommitted rows would rewrite the tracked database; a bulk write first takes the SQLite pre-op backup of the P0-P1 register rule (§9.2) through the reviewed helper `scripts/register/backup_db.sh --record <file>` (T064a): the online `.backup` (or `VACUUM INTO`) under the register lock to the ignored `docs/workable_items.db.bak-<UTC>` (covered by `docs/*.bak-*`, never an untracked file in the tracked tree), `PRAGMA integrity_check` = ok on the backup and its restore probe (the backup restored into a scratch database whose canonical dump must equal the source dump taken under the lock), both sha256 values, both row counts and the image digest recorded, and a non-zero exit, never a reported backup, when a check fails; never a hardlinked `cp -al` copy, whose hardlink shares the page file that SQLite rewrites in place, so it changes with the live database and protects nothing; then `PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)`, `scripts/register/gate.sh` and the T066 dump-and-commit procedure (DB, `docs/register/register.sql` and the documents regenerated by `scripts/register/export.sh` and `scripts/register/reconcile.sh` of T067, committed together); the large-file bound of class `generated` in the reviewed path-class table (plan owner's rules (Z) and (V); T040b; Path classes bullet) holds for every P4-P7 write up to the T576 closure sweep: 16 MiB (16,777,216 B) per file, the one register bound of this plan, for `docs/workable_items.db` and for every file under `docs/register/**` (the dump `docs/register/register.sql`, the reconciliation outputs and the regenerated exports with their HTML, PDF and DOCX siblings; T574, T577), derived with both stored copies of every description (`items.description` and `body_md`) and the measured `docs/issues` length distribution (T040b, T067); every item description a P4-P7 task writes follows the bounded description policy of T168 (at most 2,048 bytes, its Sources block included, the full ticket text staying in the source file that the Sources block cites by path and sha256), and a write that would still cross the bound stops for the held reviewed table change of T040b (a G-DATA review iteration `$EV/reviews/WP-06-r<n>.json` carrying the measured sizes), the database, the dump and every regenerated export that the raise admits declared held on that same iteration together with the raise (T040b: a path not held on the table change's own verdict is judged with the HEAD tables, and one whose result the new tables would change is refused with 20, `table_admits_unheld_path`; T178), never committed past the check. Findings are written as one `finding/1` file per finding under `$AUD/findings/` and linked to their register item through ST-REG.
- **Inherited P0-P3 rules** (abbreviation table and P0-P1 conventions, unchanged here): (a) disk headroom: every local `podman build`, `podman pull` and container run of P4-P7 (every `RUNP` and `TIC` call through the precondition built into `RUNP`, and every direct pull of a pinned tool image, for example in T317, T445 and T543) first runs `scripts/containers/disk_headroom.sh --op <op_id>` (T001), is refused with `disk_below_headroom` below the threshold and records its before and after values in `$EV/disk/<op_id>.json` (T582 alone sets `DISK_HEADROOM_OUT_DIR=.audit/out/<op_id>/disk/`, T001, so that nothing reaches the tracked tree before its strict run); images built here carry `--label project=catalogizer` and cleanup removes only labelled images and containers, never a global prune; (b) every task that writes a `docs/scripts/*.md` companion (§11.4.18) adds its row to the index `docs/scripts/README.md` (T036) in the same change, and a companion written beside its script (for example `scripts/contract/README.md`, T309) gets an index row pointing to it; (c) the ratchet baselines `scripts/repo/validate_baselines/<check>.tsv` (T040), measured over the main repository and every own-organisation repository at every depth with every key taken from the main root (plan owner's rule (W), as the secrets baseline of T040a), so that a `--repo` run of P5 scores the rows under its path and a violation that already existed in a module is a counted row, never a new key, only shrink: a task that removes counted violations lowers or deletes their rows in the same change (T448 for the main-repository rows of `check_pins.tsv`, T472 and T474 for `qa_banks.tsv` and the `challenges/helixqa-banks/` rows of the anti-bluff baseline), and no task raises a count; the rows of each own-organisation submodule repository form that repository's items (T092 registers one item per `(check, rule, repository)` group; T105), fixed or closed on a stated allowed basis by its module work (T430, T499), never lowered by another work package's main-repository task; because a `--repo` change set cannot declare the main-repository baseline file (`path_outside_repo`, T042a), a `--repo` run in which a declared file's count falls below its row is not refused: the run reports that row `baseline_lowering_owed` (key, row, new count, run id) in its report, never exit 10 and never silently (T040, T042a), and the task that ran it captures that report with its CPA run record (FP step 7); the lowering is that module work's own change, made in the main-repository change set of the pointer commit that moves that module's pin past the commit (`git merge-base --is-ancestor <commit> <new pin>`): T442 for a WP-55 layer, T581 for every pin that WP-73 moves (the constitution's pin carrying the rows of its nested engines' fixes); that change set lowers each row that those reports name to the count measured at the new pin, the same pointer change set without that lowering gives 10 at S3 (T040, T042a), and T441 and T580a check the lowering; a key that a layer fast-forward, a late catch-up, the constitution update or a CPA merge brings into a scored repository outside the declared files, and that no `baseline_lowering_owed` report covers, is reported `baseline_drift`, never a refusal of the run that meets it, and is registered through T092 at the next window, its owed action a fix of the file (a count that the drift lowered closes when a change set lowers the row to it).
- **CPA check stages** (T318, T394a, T503, T514, T535, T537, T538, T543, T564): every check that a P4-P7 task wires into CPA is a row of the check registry `scripts/repo/validate_checks.tsv` (T040; name, command, image, mode `plain`, `ratchet` or `deferred`, baseline file, and scope `files` or `changeset`), added in the change set that wires it and reviewed with it, and it is one of two kinds. A per-file check (scope `files`: it is given declared files and judges each of them; the `eslint` and `prettier` rows of T394a, `export_sync` of T535, the per-file documentation gates of T538, `squawk` of T543 and `protected_spec` of T564) takes the `files`/`types` scope of its check (plan owner's rule (Z)), and, unless its name is already in the closed check set of the check class row of the abbreviation table (as `eslint` and `prettier` are), its change set adds the check's row for every class to `scripts/repo/check_classes.tsv` (`yes` or `no`, each with its reason; T040b, plan owner's rule (V); next bullet) under the table-review form of the next bullet, with a golden-false fixture through the real stage (a file passes at a path of a class whose row says `no` and the same bytes give 10 at a `source` path; for a documentation gate, a `docs/register/Issues.md` of class `generated` whose quoted ticket text holds the word TODO passes) and its paired mutation in the T043 form (with that class row set to `yes`, the exempt file gives 10), so it never fires on a byte-exact evidence, generated or legacy-collection file; the class loader refuses with 20 (`check_unknown`, T040b) a check of scope `files` whose name has no class rows, never applying a default. A change-set check (scope `changeset`: it takes no declared-file list and judges the change set as a whole; `oasdiff_breaking` of T318, `coverage_gate` of T503, `perf_cheap` and `perf_long` of T514, `badges` of T537, the link and scope gates of T538, and `schema_doc_diff` and `tbls_diff` of T543) is outside the class table, which its registry row states. Either kind is check-only: it reads the tracked tree and writes only under its run's `$CPA_RUN` (a generator or formatter runs in its check or diff form, or on a copy under the run's `/out`, never rewriting a tracked file; the binding design of T042); and its golden-bad fixture is also run once through the real CPA stage path, not only through the check's own test, as a control needle proving that the stage can see (§11.4.201(7)(c)). A check that judges a release candidate rather than a change set is a release-seam check (P6-P7 conventions), not a CPA stage.
- **Path classes** (plan owner's rule (V); check class row of the abbreviation table; T040b, T040a, T043): which S2 and S3 checks apply to a committed file is decided by the reviewed path-class table `scripts/repo/check_exemptions.tsv` (path glob from the main-repository root to class) and the per-check class rows of `scripts/repo/check_classes.tsv`, read through `scripts/repo/check_class.sh` (T040b): `source` for every path that no row names (every check, 1,024,000 B), `generated` (`docs/workable_items.db` and `docs/register/**`, 16,777,216 B), `evidence` (anchors, deferrals, blobs and the records and captures `$EV/**/*.json`, `*.jsonl`, `*.txt`, `SHA256SUMS`, `$AUD/**/*.json`, `*.jsonl`, `*.txt`, 1,024,000 B, a ledger-named blob unbounded and listed as an OD-76 owed relocation), `evidence-ledger` (the hash-chained `$EV/ledger.jsonl` and the append-only `$EV/flake_ledger.jsonl`, under the bound that T040b derives from the projected entry count, raised only by its held reviewed table change on a G-DATA review iteration), `patches`, `governance-carrier` (`.specify/memory/constitution-appendix.md` and `submodules/constitution/Constitution.md`, the private-key check skipping only the exact lines of `scripts/repo/private_key_carriers.tsv`, 4,194,304 B), `legacy-collection` (the kept legacy collections `docs/issues/**` and the S-12 root reports: no `revision_header` check) and `fixtures`; no class exempts a file from the S2 secret fold; a path that rows of two classes match is refused with 20 (`class_ambiguous`); a table change counts only with its review: the table's header line `# review: <verdict path>` names the verdict, which holds GO in the main-repository HEAD, is declared with GO in the same change set, or holds the change as a held commit on exactly that verdict (plan owner's rule (X)), and otherwise S3 gives 10 (`class_table_unreviewed`) and the change set is judged with the tables of its HEAD; even a reviewed table change applies only to the paths held on its own verdict: every other path of the change set is judged with the HEAD tables, and a path whose result differs between the two table sets without being held on that verdict is refused with 20 (`table_admits_unheld_path`, T040b), so a task that adds a row declares every file the row admits held on the same verdict as the row. So every P4-P7 record or capture under `$EV/` and `$AUD/` with those suffixes (the contract reports of T314 to T321 under `$AUD/contract/`, the security, desktop and web reports under `$AUD/`, the qa outputs, `$EV/release_digests/`, `$EV/verify/final.json`, `$EV/pack/`) is class `evidence` with no row of its own (`$EV/flake_ledger.jsonl` is class `evidence-ledger`, T563), and the register dump, exports and reconciliation outputs are class `generated` (T574, T577). A P4-P7 task that commits other tracked files that a generator writes either writes them so that they pass every check of class `source` (for example a final newline, no trailing whitespace, a size under 1,024,000 B and, on Markdown, a revision header), which needs no row, or adds their `generated` row in its change set under the table review form above, its header naming that task's review verdict and the files the row admits declared held on that same verdict, with a golden-false fixture through the real S2 or S3 path (the same bytes at a path that no row names give 10 or 13) and its paired mutation in the T043 form (with the row dropped, the file the row admits gives 10): the rows this task list expects are T535 and T536 for the export twins and `docs/EXPORT_MANIFEST.json`, and, where their generators cannot meet the `source` checks, T523 (`docs/reference/collections/`), T537 (`docs/status/badges/`), T541 (`docs/reference/schema/`) and T546 (`docs/reference/api/routes.json`); a file above its class bound is never admitted by a raised bound without a held reviewed table change carrying the measured size (T040b). Evidence files that carry bare 40- or 64-hex strings (verdicts with `covers_runs`, findings, verifier JSON, run records, digests) pass the S2 secret fold only through the hex-hash filter of its baseline (`--exclude-secrets '^([0-9a-f]{40}|[0-9a-f]{64})$'`, recorded in the baseline's `filters_used`, T040a), never through an exemption: a fake secret assembled at run time in such a file still gives 13. A raw tool output whose suffix is none of `.json`, `.jsonl` and `.txt` (the Android lint SARIF of T426, a `.tsv`, `.xml` or `.html` report) is kept only as the ledger-named blob `$EV/blobs/<sha256>` of its `evrec` capture, never at a path of its own under `$EV/` or `$AUD/`, where it would be class `source`. Every Markdown file that a P4-P7 task creates at a path whose class applies the `revision_header` check (T040b; an `$EV/**/*.md` file is class `source` too) carries the §11.4.44 revision header (S3 `revision_header`, T040), and a P4-P7 task that edits an existing Markdown file of a class that applies the check and that has no header adds it in the same change (the basis of the T092 `legacy headerless documents` item, T576) and removes that file's `(revision_header, <path>, missing)` row in the same change set, or, in a `--repo` run, has the row reported `baseline_lowering_owed` (inherited rule (c)).
- **Owner decisions**: `BLOCKED-ON ODG-nn` names one of the 41 docs/21 §8 groups ODG-01..ODG-41 (a `research.md` decision grouped there, such as OD-21 in ODG-17 or OD-32 in ODG-15, is written `BLOCKED-ON ODG-nn (OD-nn)`; ODG-41, the closing-window decision this task list raised for WP-73 and WP-74R, has no `research.md` counterpart, has its docs/21 §8.3 row since docs/21 revision 10 and carries its markers on T582 and T595b), and `BLOCKED-ON OD-nn` one of the 15 open ungrouped decisions of docs/21 §8.6; where `research.md` §5 lists a reversible default it is quoted, otherwise the docs/21 recommendation is stated as a proposal only and the dependent step stays blocked.
- **ODG-35 working default (P5)**: under the docs/21 planning-rule-6 exception (docs/21 §8, "ODG-34 and ODG-35 in use") P5 proceeds on ODG-35's recommendation as a reversible working default: no SLA tiers, the FR-008 completion rule replaces them, and fixes are ordered by severity then exposure (docs/15 §12, §13). No `research.md` default backs it; it stays an `Operator-blocked` item the owner may overturn, which changes scheduling only, never an evidence rule. ODG-34 (P0 conventions) continues to apply.

### Standing merge review, P4 to P7 (stream ST-REV)

The merges that CPA makes in a submodule are reviewed by the reviewer of the range they merge into (Diverged repository bullet); every merge that CPA makes in the main repository from the first P4 window to the closing push is reviewed by this one standing task, the P4-P7 counterpart of the merge-file reviews that the P0-P1 conventions give ST-REV for the P0 to P2 windows and that T307 makes for P3. It belongs to no work package of docs/21 §5 and has no checkpoint of its own: a merge it has not yet accepted keeps the range above it unpushed (S6, T042).

- [ ] T308a [US7] [REVIEW] Standing merge review of the main repository (plan owner's rule (Y); T040, T042): whenever a main-repository CPA run of P4 to P7 (a commit window of any stream, a G-PIN pointer run of T442, a WP-73 root run of T581 or the closing push T595a) reports a merge commit held on `$EV/reviews/CPA-merge-<run_id>.json`, ST-REV reviews it on Opus at xhigh before the next window: the backup bundle the run report names (`git bundle verify` passes on it), the merge commit's parents (the local tip first, the newest live tip of the run second), its incoming range (each trailer-less commit of that range named by exactly one `Foreign-Commit:` line, none named twice), its tree against both sides (only the remote's commits brought in, every held change of the local range kept), and, for a merge made by a `--resolve-merge` run, the resolution record (Opus at xhigh, §11.4.211) and each resolved file; a merge made after the T566 candidate was built is also judged against that candidate: an incoming range that touches only test paths (the per-ecosystem test-path patterns of T580a (c)) is accepted on the T567 and T568 records; an incoming range whose every commit is another actor's by the WP-73 Inputs rule (no `CPA-Run` trailer) is reviewed for the rule (Y) integrity above only, and the merge-review file lists, in a `foreign_outside_candidate` field, each of those commits that changes a non-test path, with those paths, the listed commits being the main repository's ODG-41 kind in T582, T583 and T595b (WP-73 Inputs), never a reason to withhold the merge's GO, so another actor's push to the main repository never stops the push of this feature's own reviewed work; any other incoming range (a commit of this feature pushed from another clone, or an undecided commit) needs the rebuild proof of T580a (c) or a new candidate with T566 to T569 re-run; a reviewer-authored mutation recorded caught (for example: a scratch merge whose tree drops one line of a held change must be refused), as an `ev/1` `MUTATION` entry, except for a merge made after the T594 final anchor (the T595a run), whose mutation record goes only into its merge-review file and never into the ledger (as T595 records its own run); verdict GO in that merge-review file with every required `review-verdict/1` field and `covers_runs` naming the merge run (repository path, commit sha, `CPA-Run` id), committed in the main repository's HEAD by the next window or by the re-run's own declared change set (T039), after which S6 pushes the range as soon as the held commits below the merge are released by their own verdicts; a NO-GO keeps the merge held and unpushed and goes back to ST-GOV with its findings, the repair reviewed again here (never a reset, rebase or force-push, §11.4.113); inside WP-73 the one-round bound of the Diverged repository bullet applies, a further move of the main repository's remote being an ODG-41 row (WP-73 Inputs).

## Phase P5: Remediation (docs/21 §3.3 P5, §5.6)

**Entry**: P4 accepted for the contracts each fix touches; each register item root-caused (FR-008).
**Ordering**: server first, then each client against the fixed contract; within a stream by severity then exposure (docs/15 §13; the ODG-35 working default, no SLA tiers); a contract-changing fix lands with its client changes and both-sided tests in one reviewed change set. Every task follows the Fix protocol (FP) above.
**Exit**: every finding in each stream `Fixed` with custody chain or closed by an allowed basis; blocked items listed with exact reason.

### WP-55 Shared-module fixes upstream and submodule update layers (stream ST-SUB, size L, FR-006, FR-017, FR-018, SC-003, SC-009)

- [ ] T429 [US6] Preconditions (docs/11 §6.2): `scripts/repo/verify_repos.sh --fetch` (WP-03; `--fetch` fetches into the object store only, in the form T453 uses, `git fetch --no-tags --no-write-fetch-head --refmap= <remote> <branch>`, so no working tree, branch ref, remote-tracking ref or `FETCH_HEAD` moves; the T032 verifier must carry this form, because a plain `git fetch --no-tags <remote> <branch>` also updates `refs/remotes/<remote>/<branch>`; with the fetch, an upstream move is classified `LOCAL-BEHIND` instead of the unproven `UNKNOWN-DIFFERENT` a no-fetch run reports) reports `summary.unproven=0` and no failing row other than three named kinds, each listed in the preflight record: an `ahead` row, of the main repository or of a submodule holding a `--repo` held commit, whose outgoing range starts at a held commit (`Awaits-Review:` naming a verdict not yet GO in the main repository's HEAD: review-pending work of another P5 stream, which S6 does not push past before that GO), listed with each held commit's `CPA-Run` id and verdict; a `diverged` row of a submodule whose local range holds such held commits while its remote moved, listed the same way (it is integrated by its producing stream's T433 run through the diverged-repository rule, never by a layer, and T436 to T439 leave it unmoved); and a pin-drift row that exactly matches a row of `.audit/pending_pins.tsv` (path and gitlink sha, listed as pending); any other dirty (outside exceptions), ahead, diverged, drifted or unproven row stops the loop; the measurement is on the tracked tree, so the ignored CPA run directories never count, and the record cites, by sha256, the CPA run record of the run that made the measured HEAD (abbreviation table); an in-scope repository still `UNKNOWN-DIFFERENT` or `UNREACHABLE` after the fetch stops the loop with its reason (run without `--strict` here, because the `behind` rows are this work package's input; the `--strict` exit-0 run is the WP-73 condition; doc11 §6.2 precondition 1, revision 3), no other git writer (resolved by `/proc/<pid>/cmdline`), no index locks, host headroom, gate images present by digest, SSH reachability to all remote hosts; hardlinked backup `cp -al .git <backup>/catalogizer.git.mirror` plus `refs.txt`, `submodules.txt`, `head.txt` with the show-ref restore check (§9.2; git writes its index, refs and config through a lock file renamed into place and never rewrites a pack or loose object, so the hardlinked copy keeps the old files, which a SQLite database rewritten in place would not: the register is never backed up this way, P4-P5 register-writes rule); evidence: preflight `ev/1`

`--rollback <path> <old>` makes the docs/11 §6.6 rollback of a local unpushed fast-forward (`git -C <path> checkout <branch>` and `git -C <path> reset --hard <old>`, only on a clean working tree and only when `<old>` is an ancestor of HEAD that every remote of that repository holds) and then the `--after-rollback` decision, in one hold of the lock; one fetch form with docs/15 §10.3), then decide `git merge-base --is-ancestor <pin> <tip>` per fetched tip; status per remote `ok` (the pin equals or is an ancestor of a tip), `absent` (every tip of a reachable remote read and fetched and none contains the pin) or `unproven` (the remote is unreachable, an `ls-remote` or a fetch failed or timed out, or the repository is a shallow clone, `git rev-parse --is-shallow-repository` printing `true`, so ancestry cannot be decided; 1 shallow repository at plan time), never assumed present; the row takes the worst status of its remotes (`absent`, then `unproven`, then `ok`) and lists every remote with its own status; because the container source mount is read-only (`RUNP`, T007), this reachability step is a separate script `scripts/deps/pin_reachability.sh` (new), tested with the generator's fixtures through `TIC tooling unit` and run for real on the host as a read-only git step of the control plane like the WP-03 verifier's `--fetch` (docs/16 §5: it writes only objects, never a working tree, branch ref, remote-tracking ref or gitlink; ## Phase P6: Test closure, QA, performance, documentation (WP-60 to WP-65)

**Purpose**: close every applicable test-type cell, convert the HelixQA banks into asserting cases, install the performance gate, and deliver the documentation and definitions programme (docs/21 §3.3 P6).

**Entry**: WP-24 QA wrapper and validator, WP-23 applicability map and baselines, WP-38 performance baselines, WP-37 documentation baseline, WP-40 live route dump, WP-41 contract tests.

**Phase-group conventions (P6-P7)** (all paths repo-relative):

- `$SPEC` = `$FEAT` = `specs/001-full-project-audit-remediation`; `$EV` = `$SPEC/evidence` (doc06 §11 layout: `ledger.jsonl` with `ev/1` entries, `verdicts/<item-id>.json` derived only, `anchors.jsonl`, `matrix/`, `reviews/` for every `[REVIEW]` verdict, `hc/` for human-checkpoint records); `$AUD` = `$SPEC/audit`. The doc06 §11 per-application coverage folders are `$EV/coverage_baseline/<app>/` (the P2 baselines and exclusion-gate records of WP-23, read here as the ratchet floor) and `$EV/coverage_targets/<app>/` (the dated owner targets, ODG-17), both named this way because `.gitignore:139` ignores every directory named `coverage/` at any depth and no WP-09 negation covers it below the root (docs/21 IC-38). `(new)` and `.gitignore` rules as in the P4-P5 conventions.
- `TIC` = the containerized runner `scripts/test-in-container.sh <app> <lane> -- <cmd>` (created in WP-12 (T121) over the T120 wrappers; lanes `unit`, `contract`, `integration`, `e2e`, `api`, `docs`, `tooling`; doc05 §12.5). The rows of the `qa` app (`TIC qa <lane>`) dispatch to `scripts/containers/run_qa.sh` in IMG-QA, the WP-24 QA image of T211 (helixqa, Tesseract, ffmpeg, Playwright browsers); `run_qa.sh` and those rows are delivered by T212, on which every `TIC qa` call below depends (each such task names it together with T211). No test or build runs on the bare host (§11.4.173); every build of a deliverable artifact runs on the remote build host through the WP-14 mechanism (`cmd/distributed-build`, manifest checked by `scripts/build/verify_artifact.sh`, BLOCKED-ON ODG-07 when the host is unavailable), mutation runs in IMG-MUT, docs in IMG-DOCS, QA in IMG-QA.
- `CPA` = `scripts/commit-push-all.sh` (WP-04, IC-16): every commit and push of this task list goes through it, on `main` only, fast-forward only, never `--force` (§11.4.113, FR-020, FR-024); work inside a submodule repository uses its per-repository mode `CPA --repo <path>`, which moves no ancestor HEAD or gitlink and records its pending pin move in `.audit/pending_pins.tsv` (FP step 7 of the P4-P5 block); CPA writes nothing into the tracked tree, its outputs stay in the ignored `.audit/commit-push/<run_id>/`, and a task cites a run report through `evrec` (the P4-P5 commit-push outputs rule).
- Review before push, generated tracked files, commit windows and register writes: the P4-P5 rules apply unchanged (a change made before the `[REVIEW]` GO that covers it is committed as a held commit, `Awaits-Review: <verdict file>`, and pushed only once that verdict file is committed in the main repository's HEAD with GO and lists the held commit's `CPA-Run` id in `covers_runs`, the path always resolved against the main repository whichever repository holds the commit, enforced at S6; a hold on a verdict file already GO is refused with 20, `verdict_already_go`; a repository whose remote moved while it holds unpushed commits is integrated only by the CPA merge of the diverged-repository rule, held on its own merge-review file `$EV/reviews/CPA-merge-<run_id>.json` and reviewed by the reviewer that bullet names (the standing merge review T308a for the main repository), never by a merge made by hand; a container writes generated tracked files under `/out` and a host step copies them with matching sha256; uncommitted work of parallel streams is committed in the P0-P1 commit windows by the conductor (ST-GOV), and no stream declares another stream's files; every mutating `$WI` or `sqlite3` command on `docs/workable_items.db` runs through `scripts/register/locked.sh` (T064) in IMG-TESTUTIL, bulk writes with the SQLite `.backup` pre-op backup through `scripts/register/backup_db.sh` (T064a; never `cp -al`) and the T066 dump-and-commit procedure; CPA check stages registered in `scripts/repo/validate_checks.tsv` with their scope and, for a per-file check, its class rows, check-only and needle-proven through the real stage path; the inherited disk-headroom, `docs/scripts/README.md` row and ratchet-baseline rules and the path-class rules of the P4-P5 block apply too).
- "Three-run evidence" = three runs with identical canonical hashes recorded in `$EV/ledger.jsonl` (doc06 §5, SC-003). "Paired mutation" = a mutation observed to turn the test RED, recorded with `mutation: {operator, location, result: "caught"}` (doc05 §8.2).
- `[REVIEW]` tasks are pure reviews owned by stream ST-REV on Opus at xhigh (§11.4.209), never by the producing stream (§11.4.240), iterate to zero blocking findings (§11.4.134), record at least one reviewer-authored mutation where a test or gate is reviewed (§11.4.194(6)(d)), and write their verdict to `$EV/reviews/<WP>[-<item>][-r<n>].json` (a merge commit that CPA makes being held on its own `$EV/reviews/CPA-merge-<run_id>.json`, P4-P5 Diverged repository bullet) in the `review-verdict/1` format of the P4-P5 block (`schema`, `verdict`, `blocking_findings`, `covers_runs`, `model` and `effort`, every field the schema requires); any fix goes back to the producing stream.
- **Release seam**: CPA has no release mode (T042). A check that this task list calls a release-seam check (the performance verdict of T514, the escape-ratchet and catchability gates of T554) is a standalone script with its own exit code that judges the candidate fingerprint recorded by T566, never a CPA stage; it runs at two named points: T569 (every release-seam check on the final candidate fingerprint, the performance verdict included) and T582 (every release-seam check re-run on that fingerprint before the final verification is accepted, its output written under `.audit/out/<op_id>/` and cited in T582's `ev/1` entry); a refusal at any of them blocks the candidate exactly as a FAIL does (§11.4.135), and an absent verdict is a refusal, never a pass.
- A test that needs an absent credential, device, host or service is recorded `blocked-unavailable` with the exact reason (FR-025); it never counts as passing and is never replaced by a mock or a skip.

under the options below such a commit is (a) an explained exception `integrated, outside the candidate` naming the commit, (b) a breach reported by name when it was pushed inside the freeze window and otherwise handled as (c), or (c) a new candidate built by T566 with T567 to T569 re-run on it; options as docs/21 lists them: (a) T582 and T595b compare against the reference tips and list every later commit that the rule below classifies as another actor's as an explained exception naming the commit, (b) an owner-agreed push freeze on the own-organisation remotes for the window from T579a to T595b, a move inside it being a breach reported by name, (c) re-running the closing steps until no upstream moves (the only option under which T581 runs a second merge-and-review round for a repository; P4-P5 Diverged repository bullet); docs/21 recommendation: option (a), because the constitution's `main` moves several times a day (read with `git ls-remote`: `be06384` earlier on 2026-10-03, T080; `0c1c588` at 20:25Z in the round-8 review; `b195981` at about 20:38Z for revision 9; `00697b5` at 21:20Z in the round-9 review; `e971a01` at 21:41Z on its `gitflic` remote for this revision), so a strict check days later, after HC-7, fails with near certainty and its failure would reopen WP-73 and HC-7 in a loop; until answered, a failure of that kind alone is reported `blocked: ODG-41`, never a pass and never a reopening loop (the `BLOCKED-ON ODG-41` markers of T582 and T595b, partial proceed: the strict run and every other assertion proceed, only those rows wait)). The verifier is the single WP-03 verifier (IC-17). Pass condition everywhere in this package: `scripts/repo/verify_repos.sh --strict` exit 0, `summary.failing=0`, `summary.unproven=0` (data-model §9, docs/21 §5.8), or, once the owner has chosen ODG-41 option (a), a non-zero exit whose every failing row is an explained exception naming its foreign-only commits, with A-5, A-6 and, for the root row, A-7 recomputed by T583 without those rows and passing; and, in either case, every `foreign_outside_candidate` commit of the main repository (T308a) handled under ODG-41 as T582 states; measured on the tracked tree: CPA writes nothing into it (T042), its run directories under `.audit/commit-push/` are ignored and never count, and a measurement cites, by its report sha256, the CPA run record of the run that made the measured HEAD (abbreviation table); a repository whose remote moved shows `UNKNOWN-DIFFERENT` without a fetch and fails as unproven, and a diagnostic re-run with `--fetch` (object store only) classifies it as `LOCAL-BEHIND`. That diagnostic re-run also names the new upstream commits of such a row, each classified from tracked records only, because run directories are per worktree, ignored and retention-swept (T042) and decide nothing: a commit was made by this feature when its `CPA-Run` trailer names a run id listed in a CPA run record captured into `$EV/ledger.jsonl` (the ledger read at the main repository's HEAD and, for commits pushed from another clone of this feature, at the fetched tip of the main repository's remote; FP step 7 makes every `--repo` commit run capture its record, and T582 captures every T581 run); by another actor when it carries no `CPA-Run` trailer; and a commit whose `CPA-Run` id no captured record lists is undecided and fails as an ordinary row, never excepted, so a missing capture can only make the check stricter.

---

## Dependencies and execution order

- Phase order is strict at the exit gates: **P0 -> P1 -> P2 -> P3 -> P4 -> P5 -> P6 -> P7**. The gate evidence for each phase is the `Checkpoint` line of its last work package plus the human checkpoints HC-1..HC-n in docs/21 section 3.3.
- Inside a phase, work packages follow the Inputs column of docs/21 section 5 (diagram in docs/21 section 5.9, acyclic, checked by script). Tasks marked `[P]` inside one work package, and work packages with disjoint Inputs, may run concurrently, bounded by the host limits in docs/21 section 4.2 (60 percent memory ceiling, process-limit headroom, at most 6 concurrent agents).
- Producer and reviewer streams are separate: `[REVIEW]` tasks belong to ST-REV and never to the stream that produced the work (WP-39R, WP-74R).
- Owner-blocked tasks (`BLOCKED-ON ODG-nn`) are skipped, not guessed; the autonomous loop continues with every unblocked task and records the block (docs/21 section 8).

- **Forward references**: 7 tasks name a later task as an explicit dependency (T027 after T069, T042 after T089, T052 after T069, T054 after T069, T275 after T279, T469 after T493, T534 after T542). Such a task is scheduled after its dependency regardless of its position in this document; the explicit "depends on"/"after" graph over all tasks was checked by script and is acyclic (loose phrases such as "after the read-only source mount" or "for example T011" are narrative, not dependencies).

## Implementation strategy

1. **MVP / first deliverable**: P0 and P1 (verifier, evidence recorder, register, pinned containers), then P2 baselines. After P2 exit the register contains every known item and the baselines exist, which already satisfies US1 on its own.
2. **Incremental delivery**: each later phase ends at a checkpoint that is independently demonstrable (audit accepted at G-AUDIT, contract layer, remediation per component, test closure, final verification).
3. **Parallel team strategy**: streams ST-INFRA, ST-QA, ST-GOV, ST-REG, ST-SEC, ST-API, ST-WEB, ST-DESK, ST-AND, ST-SUB, ST-DOC, ST-PERF and ST-REV work in parallel once their phase gate opens.

## Notes

- Task IDs T001-T595 were renumbered sequentially in rev 4 (rev 3 had T001-T592; rev 4 inserts one task, the HC-2 record T222 of the new P2 exit gate, so every later id moved up by one); every `Tnnn` reference inside descriptions, `depends on` clauses, ranges and the forward-reference note was remapped with them. Rev 3 had renumbered after inserting its own tasks and moving the P0 exit gate and the WP-01 register import behind their dependencies.
- Paths, scripts and tool facts proposed by the plan and not yet verified are labelled `(new)` or UNCONFIRMED in the task text; nothing in this list has been executed.
- Every FR (FR-001..FR-025) and SC (SC-001..SC-012) traces to at least one work package in docs/21 section 6; the coverage of this task list against that matrix is checked in the review pass.
